# Health AI Systems Database (Monetizable Concepts)

Generated database of health-focused AI system concepts with monetization models.
All entries are conceptual; validate market demand, evidence, and compliance before commercialization.

---
## System 0001 — Medical Imaging AI Copilot
Domain: Medical Imaging
Subdomain: Triage optimization
Target Users: Care coordinators
Buyer Persona: Clinic director
Core Problem: Delayed diagnosis
AI Capability: Predictive risk modeling
Primary Data Inputs: Medical claims and billing records
Key Outputs: Automated care plans
Workflow Position: Diagnosis to treatment
Monetization Model: Per-member-per-month
Pricing Basis: Tiered by clinic size
Sales Channels: Health system partnerships
Implementation Stack: FHIR/HL7 integrations
Compliance Notes: GDPR where applicable
Differentiators: Transparent audit trails
ROI Drivers: Lower readmission rates
Primary KPIs: Readmission rate
Risk Notes: Model drift over time
Expansion Paths: Expand to new specialties
